Re-anchored on MAST (arXiv:2503.13657) and ETCLOVG (arXiv:2606.06324) failure taxonomies; supersedes the trivial '5 Whys' spine with the Observe→Isolate→Simulate→Evaluate 4-step pattern, retaining 5-Whys as a Simulate-phase prompt heuristic. v0.3.0 (2026-07-05): adds Phase 1.5 'fix authored but never merged to origin/main' detection recipe + replaces naive `deploy.sh --s… ([jleechanorg/jleechanbrain](https://github.com/jleechanorg/jleechanbrain/tree/HEAD/skills/harness-postmortem) · ★2)
- **harness-pr**: 로컬 검증을 실행하고 PR 옵션을 질문으로 정한 뒤, PR 작성 규칙에 따라 본문을 처음부터 쓴 초안을 보여주고 승인 후 push와 PR 생성까지 수행한다. 열린 PR에 push한 뒤 본문을 지금 커밋에 맞게 고칠 때도 쓴다. "PR 올려줘", "PR 만들어줘", "풀리퀘 생성", "PR 본문 고쳐줘" 요청에 사용한다. ([Qello-Labs/qello-knowledge](https://github.com/Qello-Labs/qello-knowledge/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/harness-pr) · 2 copies)
- **harness-pr-ops**: Use while DRIVING a harness change onto trunk — opening/merging PRs, especially several at once (a roadmap or /retro batch), or the moment a guard unexpectedly BLOCKS a git/gh/bin-harness command mid-flow. Encodes the operating mechanics the enforcement guards + GitHub impose that aren't obvious until they bite: stacked-PR merge order, running locked-path commands cleanly, and what to expect at the human gate. Pairs with harness-pr (the PR template), windows-host-paths (token mechanics), worktree. ([GhostlyGawd/recursive-harness](https://github.com/GhostlyGawd/recursive-harness/tree/HEAD/skills/harness-pr-ops) · ★3)
- **harness-pr-reviewer**: Checks a submitted PR against the environment's baseline assets (CLAUDE.md, memory, naming, asset classification) and attaches a review comment with a merge recommendation. 5 steps — diff read, 8-area consistency check, self-catch, comment, merge recommendation. ([chrono-meta/forge-harness](https://github.com/chrono-meta/forge-harness/tree/HEAD/plugins/fh-meta/skills/harness-pr-reviewer) · ★33)
- **harness-prd**: Turn the current conversation context into a GSD-compatible PRD with WHAT/HOW constraints, optionally writing to .harness/phases/01-discuss/CONTEXT.md. Use when user wants to create a PRD from the current context. ([ClydeShen/harness-skill](https://github.com/ClydeShen/harness-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/harness-prd))
- **harness-prd**: Interviews the user to design a stateful-agent-harness ProcessSpec (a state machine of agents, skills, gates, artifacts); use for "build an agent process for X", "spec out an agent pipeline". NOT for code work-breakdowns (wbs-prd). ([coachlou/ambient-library](https://github.com/coachlou/ambient-library/tree/HEAD/library/harness-prd) · 3 copies)
- **harness-prd**: Stage 01 — turn an approved idea into a PRD with numbered testable REQ-### requirements, non-goals, and a data inventory, targeting gate G1. ([JanusCoetzee/AI_WF_Harness](https://github.com/JanusCoetzee/AI_WF_Harness/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/harness-prd))
- **harness-pre-commit-review**: &gt; Lightweight pre-commit quality gate — mechanical checks first, AI review second. Fast feedback before code leaves your machine. ([Intense-Visions/harness-engineering](https://github.com/Intense-Visions/harness-engineering/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/claude-code/harness-pre-commit-review) · ★23)
- **harness-pre-loop-checkin**: Use for a read-only GO/NO-GO check of the harness backlog BEFORE starting an unattended loop run — phrases like "is the backlog ready to run", "check before I start the loop", "pre-flight the harness", "/pre-loop-checkin". Reports needs-human blockers, session hygiene (dirty tree / running loop / lock), dependency short-circuits, and per-task definition quality (facets, spec, scope), then a plain GO / NO-GO verdict. Strictly READ-ONLY — it never edits, commits, or pushes anything. Requires the harness scaffolded. ([RyanMKrol/LocalJobs](https://github.com/RyanMKrol/LocalJobs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/harness-pre-loop-checkin))
